Let's consider designing a blocked algorithm that's correct and efficient with MPI rows and CUDA.\n\nBlocked Floyd-Warshall with block size B:\nClassic blocked algorithm:\n```\nfor kk in 0..n step B:\n    // Phase 1: update diagonal block D[kk:kk+B, kk:kk+B] using standard FW for k in kk..kk+B\n    // Phase 2: update blocks in pivot row/column? \n    // Phase 3: update remaining blocks using pivot block\n```\nWith row distribution, pivot block rows [kk,kk+B) may be on one or more ranks (B chosen maybe divide equally). That's phase 2: update pivot column (all rows, columns panel) using diagonal block. Then phase 3 update remaining blocks using pivot row and pivot column. So blocked algorithm:\n1. Diagonal block update (panel rows, panel cols), sequential over k in panel.\n2. Pivot column update: for all rows i not in panel, for each k in panel? Standard approach computes block for column panel using current D[i][panel] and D[panel][panel].\n3. Pivot row update: for rows in panel, columns outside: dist[k][j].\n4. Update remaining block: dist[i][j] = min(dist[i][j], min_{k in panel} dist[i][k] + dist[k][j]).\n\nIf panel rows/cols are updated correctly, then min-plus over B intermediates in any order. This is standard. Communication: panel needed globally after phase1. For phase2, non-panel rows need dist[i][panel] (local columns) and panel block. For phase3, need updated pivot row (rows panel all cols) globally. For phase4, need updated pivot column dist[i][panel] (local) and updated pivot row dist[panel][j] (global). So broadcast panel block (B*B) and pivot row (B*n) after phases. Wait phase2 updates column panel for all rows, including non-panel rows; its results stay local (each rank's rows). phase3 updates pivot row columns outside, must be done by panel owner and then broadcast full panel rows. Actually panel rows after phase1 and phase3 (pivot row) become the global panel rows used in phase4. blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int ii = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (ii >= B || j >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t row =          path[row + j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(S + bestT);\n        }\n    }\n\n    tmp[row + j] = best;\n}\n\n__global__ void fwRemainingKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,\n bcastUnsigned(unsigned int* data, size_t count, int root, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    while (count > 0) {\n        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(count, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));\n        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(data, chunk, MPI_UNSIGNED, root, comm));\n        data += chunk;\n        count -= static_cast<size_t>(chunk);\n    }\n}\n\nstatic void runDiagonalUpdate(unsigned int* d_dist, unsigned int* d_path,\n                              const size_t n, const size_t S, const size_t B) {\n    if (B == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n    const               unsigned int* h_panelD, unsigned int* h_panelRows) {\n    const bool multi = (numRanks > 1);\n    const dim3 block(16, 16);\n\n    for (int b = 0; b < numRanks; ++b) {\n        const       }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        if (!multi) {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        if (rank == b) {\n            runDiagonalUpdate(d_dist, d_path, numNodes, S, B);\n
